\noindent Notation as in your files; $\mathcal N=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^3}\frac{g^4}{16\pi^5}\frac{1}{k^+}$, $K(a,b,z)=\mathcal K(a-z)\cdot\mathcal K(b-z)$, $U^{bc}=U^{cb}$. Groups I and III are taken with their c.c., Group II without, everywhere. In the rows: black = your \texttt{BFKL.tex}, \new{blue} = added, \old{red} = to be removed. The remaining terms are in \texttt{Remaining\_rows.pdf}. The same analysis from your Group I, II, III and \texttt{4rho.tex} directly: \texttt{full\_check/Checks\_JIMWLK\_BFKL.pdf} and \texttt{full\_check/Remaining\_RC\_DGLAP\_finite.pdf}.

\section{Answer}\label{sec:answer}
\paragraph{BFKL is there.} Your cross sections give
\begin{equation}
\frac{d^3N_{\rm BFKL}}{d^3k}=\mathcal N\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\;{\rm BFKL}\otimes{\rm LO},
\end{equation}
your App.~E formula, Eq.~\eqref{eq:BFKLfinal}. Only $\vee>k^+$ and $p^+>\Lambda$ are used, with $\Lambda\to0$ wherever possible and $\vee$ exact. Every step is an exact identity, Sec.~\ref{sec:exact}; nothing is expanded or dropped, and what is not $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ stays, exactly, in the remaining terms.

\paragraph{Your Group I, II, III files} are correct (Sec.~\ref{sec:groups}). The one change that matters is E1, in Group I Row V 2b UV. The others are typos and measures.

\paragraph{Where the BFKL terms are.}
\begin{itemize}
\item In explicit logarithms of \texttt{Other\_terms.tex}, e.g.\ $\log(\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1)=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}+\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})$.
\item In line 1 of Group III Rows III, V, VI and Group II Row IV, the $p^+$ (resp.\ $\xi$) integral. Three exact identities bring them out:
\begin{itemize}
\item the subtraction of the plus prescription gives $-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, which cancels the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ of line 2 exactly (I2);
\item the 2-D Fourier transform of the $y'$ integral at $y'=z$ gives the terms (B), with $\int_0^{\vee-k^+}\frac{dp^+}{p^++k^+}=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ (I3);
\item the Fourier transform of $\log(y'-y)^2$ gives $H_4$ (I4).
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}
The test that the split is complete: the remaining terms stay finite for $\vee\to\infty$. This fixes the coefficient of $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ uniquely.

\paragraph{Changes to your files.}
\begin{itemize}
\item \texttt{BFKL.tex}: remove only Group I Row V 2b UV (\old{red}, E1). Your two lines of Group II Row IV are correct: they are (I2) and (I4).
\item Add (\new{blue}): the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ parts still in \texttt{Other\_terms.tex}; and, for Group III Rows III, V, VI, the terms $-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ and (B) from line 1.
\item \texttt{Other\_terms.tex}: line 1 of Group III Rows III, V, VI and Group II Row IV in its exact rewritten form, and $\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})$ in line 2 (\texttt{Remaining\_rows.pdf}).
\end{itemize}

\paragraph{Result.}
\begin{itemize}
\item Row by row, BFKL list $+$ remaining terms $=$ the row minus its JIMWLK part. The pointwise terms agree to $2.5\times10^{-9}$ ($O(\Lambda)$ at $\Lambda=10^{-9}$). The rewriting of line 1 is checked numerically, with the $p^+$, $\xi$ integrals done, to $3.7\times10^{-15}$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:exact}).
\item The 41 three-$\rho$ terms \textbf{cancel}, to $3.5\times10^{-16}$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:three}). Your \texttt{BFKL.tex} alone leaves $0.39$.
\item The list equals, row by row, the list whose two-$\rho$ terms add up to \textbf{BFKL$\otimes$LO} exactly (to $8.8\times10^{-17}$; 9 kernel structures, 109 colour structures, 0 differences; Sec.~\ref{sec:two}).
\item The list has no pole. Your Group II Row IV line 1 and the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ part of line 2 cancel, poles included ($2.9\times10^{-16}$).
\item The result is your commented ``BFKL contribution'' to $2.1\times10^{-16}$. In colour-singlet form it is your App.~E formula (Sec.~\ref{sec:final}).
\end{itemize}

\paragraph{Correction of my previous version.} There I split only the explicit logarithms and kept every $p^+$, $\xi$ integral untouched. Then the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ inside line 1 stays in the remaining terms, which grow with $\vee$. That split is not complete, and that is why BFKL$\otimes$LO did not come out.

(B) and $H_4$ are not approximations. They follow from the exact identities (I2)--(I5). Earlier I described them through the region of large $p^+$; that was a description, not a step of the calculation.

\section{The exact identities}\label{sec:exact}
Nothing is expanded: each line below is an identity, and every remainder is kept in \texttt{Remaining\_rows.pdf}.

\paragraph{(I1) Explicit logarithms.} $\log(\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1)=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}+\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})$, $\log\frac{(\vee-k^+)A+k^+B}{\Lambda A+k^+B}=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}+\log\frac{(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})A+\frac{k^+}{\vee}B}{B}+O(\Lambda)$, \dots\ (the table is in \texttt{LL\_terms.pdf}, Sec.~2).

\paragraph{(I2) The subtraction of the plus prescription.} With your $\left[\frac{1}{p^+}\right]_+f=\frac{f(p^+)-f(0)}{p^+}$, define $\left[\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}\right]_+f\equiv\frac{k^+[f(p^+)-f(0)]}{p^+(p^++k^+)}$. Since $\frac{1}{p^+}=\frac{1}{p^++k^+}+\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}$ and $\int_0^{\vee-k^+}\frac{dp^+}{p^++k^+}=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$,
\begin{equation}
\int_0^{\vee-k^+}dp^+\left[\frac{1}{p^+}\right]_+f(p^+)=\int_0^{\vee-k^+}dp^+\left(\left[\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}\right]_++\frac{1}{p^++k^+}\right)f(p^+)-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}.
\label{eq:I2}
\end{equation}
For $\xi$, with your $\frac{d\xi}{\xi^2}\frac{\xi}{(1-\xi)_+}g=\frac{d\xi}{\xi(1-\xi)}[g(\xi)-g(1)]$, define $\frac{d\xi}{\xi^2}\frac{\xi^2}{(1-\xi)_+}g\equiv\frac{d\xi}{1-\xi}[g(\xi)-g(1)]$. Then $\frac{1}{\xi(1-\xi)}=\frac{1}{\xi}+\frac{1}{1-\xi}$ gives
\begin{equation}
\int_{\frac{k^+}{\vee}}^1\frac{d\xi}{\xi^2}\frac{\xi}{(1-\xi)_+}g=\int_{\frac{k^+}{\vee}}^1\frac{d\xi}{\xi^2}\Big(\xi+\frac{\xi^2}{(1-\xi)_+}\Big)g-g(1)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}.
\label{eq:I2xi}
\end{equation}
Your line 2 is $f(0)\big[\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log(\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1)\big]$ (resp.\ $g(1)[\dots]$). It came from this subtraction, so its $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ and the $-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ above cancel exactly, and $\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})$ is left. In Group II Row IV, $-g(1)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ is your \texttt{BFKL.tex} line 1.

\paragraph{(I3) The $y'$ integral at $y'=z$.} In line 1 of Group III Rows III(1), V, VI, take the $-\frac{1}{k^+}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}$ term; in Row III(2), the $-\frac{p^+}{k^{+2}}$ term. Its coefficient tends to a constant at large $p^+$. Write
\begin{equation*}
G^{k'}(y')=\frac{(x'-y')^{k'}}{(x'-y')^2}\times\big[\text{colour bracket with }U(y')\big]=G^{k'}(z)+\big[G^{k'}(y')-G^{k'}(z)\big].
\end{equation*}
With $\int_u e^{-iq\cdot u}\frac{u^m}{u^2}=-2\pi i\frac{q^m}{q^2}$ and $q=k\frac{p^++k^+}{k^+}$, exactly,
\begin{equation}
\int_{y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}e^{-ik\cdot\frac{p^+}{k^+}(y'-z)}\frac{(y'-z)^m}{(y'-z)^2}\,G^{k'}(z)=\frac{k^+}{p^++k^+}\int_{w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\frac{(w'-z)^m}{(w'-z)^2}\,G^{k'}(z).
\label{eq:I3}
\end{equation}
For Rows III(1), V, VI: $\int_0^{\vee-k^+}\frac{dp^+}{2\pi}\big(-\frac{1}{k^+}\big)\frac{k^+}{p^++k^+}=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, exactly. This is (B), with nothing left over.

\paragraph{(I5) Row III(2).} With $A=(x-z)^2$, $B=(x-w)^2$,
\begin{equation}
-\frac{p^+}{k^{+2}}\,\frac{k^+}{p^++k^+}\,\frac{1}{p^+A+k^+B}=-\frac{1}{k^+A\,(p^++k^+)}+\frac{B}{A\,(p^++k^+)(p^+A+k^+B)}.
\label{eq:I5}
\end{equation}
The first term gives (B). The second falls like $\frac{1}{p^{+2}}$ and stays in the remaining terms.

\paragraph{(I4) Group II Row IV.} In the $\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}$ term of line 1, write $H(y,y')=H(y,y)+[H(y,y')-H(y,y)]$, with $H(y,y')=\frac{(x-y)\cdot(x'-y')}{(x-y)^2(x'-y')^2}[U^{e'c}(y')-U^{e'c}(x')][U^{ec}(y)-U^{ce}(x)]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^e(x)$. The constant part of the pole bracket gives $\delta^{(2)}(k/\xi)=0$ for $k\neq0$, and $\int_{y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y'-y)\frac{1}{\xi}}\log\frac{(y'-y)^2\mu^2}{4}=-\frac{4\pi\xi^2}{k^2}$. Also $\int_{\frac{k^+}{\vee}}^1d\xi\,\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1+\frac{k^+}{\vee}$ and $\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\mathcal K(w-z)\cdot\mathcal K(w'-z)=\frac{4\pi^2}{k^2}$. So the $H(y,y)$ part is, exactly,
\begin{equation}
\frac{g^2}{4\pi^2}\frac{N_c}{(2\pi)^3}\frac{g^2}{2\pi^2}\frac{1}{k^+}\,\frac{d_\perp}{2}\,\frac{4\pi}{k^2}\Big[\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1+\frac{k^+}{\vee}\Big]\int_{x,x',z}H(z,z)
=\Big[\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1+\frac{k^+}{\vee}\Big]\,H_4\Big|_{\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\to1},
\label{eq:I4}
\end{equation}
at $d_\perp=2$ (no pole multiplies it). Its $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ term is your $H_4$, Group II Row IV line 2.

\paragraph{The split is complete.} After these steps every remaining $p^+$ ($\xi$) integrand falls at least like $\frac{1}{p^{+2}}$ after the transverse integrals:
\begin{itemize}
\item $G(y')-G(z)$ and $H(y,y')-H(y,y)$ vanish at the coincident point;
\item the $\frac{1}{p^++k^+}$ and $\left[\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}\right]_+$ terms carry the $\frac{k^+}{p^++k^+}$ of (I3);
\item the remaining terms are convergent oscillating integrals.
\end{itemize}
So the remaining terms stay finite for $\vee\to\infty$, and the coefficient of $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ is unique. $\vee$ stays exact in the remaining terms.

\paragraph{Numerical checks} (\texttt{exact\_check.py}). Two configurations, $\vee=20k^+$, the $p^+$ and $\xi$ integrals done numerically, pointwise in the transverse points:
\begin{itemize}
\item line 1 $=$ (rewritten line 1) $+$ ($G(y')-G(z)$ term) $+$ ($-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$) $+$ ($G(z)$ term), for Group III Rows III(1), III(2), V, VI; and line 1 $=$ (rewritten line 1) $+$ (your line 1) for Group II Row IV: $\le3.7\times10^{-15}$;
\item the $G(z)$ term in the form \eqref{eq:I3}, $p^+$ integral done, $=$ (B) ($+$ the second term of \eqref{eq:I5} in Row III(2)): $\le3.4\times10^{-15}$;
\item your Group II Row IV line 1 $+$ the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ part of line 2 $=0$: $5.5\times10^{-16}$;
\item the Fourier transforms: $\int_0^\infty J_1(qr)\,dr\cdot q$ with regulator $e^{-ar}$, $a=0.1,0.03,0.01$: 0.9857, 0.9957, 0.9986; 0.9947, 0.9984, 0.9995; $2\pi\int_0^\infty rJ_0(qr)\log r^2dr\big/(-\frac{4\pi}{q^2})$ with $e^{-ar^2}$: 1.0350, 1.0169, 1.0083, 0.9997; 1.0045, 1.0022, 1.0011, 1.0000 (both $\to1$ as $a\to0$; $q=0.7,1.9$).
\end{itemize}

\section{Group I (with c.c.)}\label{sec:BgI}
\subsection{Row I}
Only four $\rho$\textquotesingle s (Sec.~\ref{sec:four}).
\subsection{Row II}
\paragraph{Row II, first part.}
No BFKL term. The only $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ is in $\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, whose coefficient depends on $\vee$. For every $\vee>k^+$ it is bounded, $0\le-\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\le\log^22$, with the maximum at $\vee=2k^+$. It stays in the remaining terms, as in your \texttt{Other\_terms.tex}.
\paragraph{Row II, second part, $d^3N_{2a}$.}
As you have it.
\noindent Your \texttt{BFKL.tex}:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,a}}{d^{3}k} & =  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{i\,g^{4}\,f^{hbc}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}\,
\int_{w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\int_{x,y,x',z}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\,\Bigg[\frac{(x-w)^{i}}{(x-w)^{2}}\, \frac{(w-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(w-z)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & - \frac{(y-w)^{i}}{(y-w)^{2}}\, \frac{(w-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(w-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\Bigg]\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\Big]\times\, \Big[U^{ah}(w)\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}\Big] \notag \\
& -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{i\,g^{4}\,f^{hbc}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)\,
\int_{w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\int_{x,y,x',z}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\,\Bigg[\frac{(x-w)^{i}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\log\frac{(w-y)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}-\frac{(y-w)^{i}}{(y-w)^{2}}\,\log\frac{(w-x)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}\Bigg]\times\notag \\ & \Big[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\Big]\times\, \Big[U^{ah}(w)\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}\Big] \label{eq:Byour:G1II2a}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row II, second part, $d^3N_{2b}$.}
No BFKL term ($R^i$ depends on $\vee$ only through $\frac{k^+}{\vee}$).

& \nc  - \frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\new{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-w)\, (y-z)\cdot (x-z)\, (x-w)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2} - (x-z)^{2}\right]\, (x-z)^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\

\paragraph{Row V, second part, $d^3N_{2b\,\rm UV}$.}
\textbf{Remove (\old{red}).} Row V has $0<p^+<k^+$: no $\vee$ and no $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$. Before ``Setting $\Lambda\to0$'' your bracket is $2\log\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}-2\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}+2\log(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+})-\frac{11}{6}=2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+2\log(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+})-\frac{11}{6}$. The $2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ is in your JIMWLK terms, and $2\log(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+})-\frac{11}{6}$ is in \texttt{Other\_terms.tex}. This line comes from the bracket $\big[\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}+\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}\big]-2\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ after ``Setting $\Lambda\to0$'', which lacks a factor 2 on the first bracket (E1).

\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}\left[\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\gamma_{E}+\log\frac{(y-z)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\right]
\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \rc   \Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(z)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y). \label{eq:Byour:G1V2bUV}
\end{align}

& \nc  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{16\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\int_{w,x',w',z,y,x} e^{-ik\cdot (w'-w)}\,\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (y-w)\,(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(w'-z)^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\
& \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag \\
& \nc  \times\left[U^{ac}(w)\, \{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\right] \label{eq:Bline1:G3III1}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row III, second part.}
\new{Added}: (i) and (ii) as in the first part (they cancel), and (iii) (B) from the $-\frac{p^+}{k^{+2}}$ term, Eqs.~\eqref{eq:I3}, \eqref{eq:I5}.
\noindent \new{From \texttt{Other\_terms.tex}} (i):
\begin{align}
 \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =  \nc   -\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\new{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}}\,\old{\int_{w,y',z,y,x}}\,\new{\int_{w,x',y',z,x}}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')\,(z-w)\cdot (y'-z)}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  +\frac{(x-z)\cdot (y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\bigg]\, \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, 
    U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times\notag \\ & \nc   \left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right] \label{eq:Bnew:G3III2}
\end{align}

\noindent \new{From line 1, by the exact identities} (ii), (iii):
\begin{align}
 \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =  \nc +\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\new{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}}\,\old{\int_{w,y',z,y,x}}\,\new{\int_{w,x',y',z,x}}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')\,(z-w)\cdot (y'-z)}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  +\frac{(x-z)\cdot (y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\bigg]\, \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, 
    U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times\notag \\ & \nc   \left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\int_{w,x',w',z,x} e^{-ik\cdot (w'-w)}\,\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(x'-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\
& \nc  \times\bigg[\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (z-w)}{(w'-z)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (x-w)}{2\,(w'-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag \\
& \nc  \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag \\
& \nc  \times\left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right] \label{eq:Bline1:G3III2}
\end{align}

\subsection{Row V}
\paragraph{Row V.}
\new{Added}: (i), (ii), (iii) as in Row III: (i) and (ii) cancel, and (B) comes from the $-\frac{1}{k^+}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}$ term.
\noindent \new{From \texttt{Other\_terms.tex}} (i):
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =  \nc  -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\new{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}} \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\right] \label{eq:Bnew:G3V}
\end{align}

\noindent \new{From line 1, by the exact identities} (ii), (iii):
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =  \nc +\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\new{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}} \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\int_{w,w',z,y,x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot (w'-w)}\,\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (y-w)\,(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(w'-z)^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\
& \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\left[U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\right] \label{eq:Bline1:G3V}
\end{align}

    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =  \nc   -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\new{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}}\, \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)-U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\right] \label{eq:Bnew:G3VI}

\noindent \new{From line 1, by the exact identities} (ii), (iii):

    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =  \nc +\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\new{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}}\, \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)-U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\right] \notag \\

\section{Four \texorpdfstring{$\rho$}{rho}'s}\label{sec:four}
Your \texttt{BFKL.tex}, unchanged: the three-$\rho$ remainders $d^3N_a$, $d^3N_b$ (they enter Sec.~\ref{sec:three}) and the two-$\rho$ lists $d^3N_1$, $d^3N_2$. Numerically, $d^3N_1+d^3N_2$ equals the four-$\rho$ two-$\rho$ terms that entered the checked BFKL sum (Eqs.~\eqref{eq:P12}, \eqref{eq:P13} below) to $3.2\times10^{-15}$ (charges symmetrized, with c.c.).
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{a\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & = -\,\frac{i}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{3}}\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}} \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,{\color{blue}\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}}\,\int_{x,y,z,x^{\prime},w,w^{\prime}}e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \Bigg[\notag \\ &  \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\, \mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{d}(x') \rho^{a}(y) \rho^{c}(x)\,U^{d e}(w') U^{e b}(w)\, {\color{red} \alpha} \nonumber\\
    &  -\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\, \mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{e}(x') \rho^{a}(y) \rho^{c}(x)\, U^{d b}(w) U^{e d}(x')\, {\color{red} \alpha}\nonumber\\
    & + \mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\, \mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{c}(x') \rho^{a}(y) \rho^{e}(x)\,U^{d b}(w') U^{e d}(x)\, {\color{orange} \alpha'} \nonumber\\
    & -\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\, \mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{c}(x') \rho^{a}(y) \rho^{e}(x) U^{b d}(w') U^{e d}(w)\, {\color{orange} \alpha'}\Bigg]
\end{align}
\begin{align}
      \frac{d^{3}N_{b\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{i}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{3}}\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}} \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,{\color{blue}\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}}\,\int_{x,y,z,x^{\prime},w,w^{\prime}}e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \Bigg[\notag \\ & - \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\, \mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{d}(x') \rho^{h}(y) \rho^{e}(x) U^{d b}(w') U^{e c}(x) U^{a h}(y)\, {\color{red} \alpha}\nonumber\\
    &+ \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{d}(x') \rho^{e}(x) \rho^{h}(y) U^{d c}(x') U^{e b}(w) U^{a h}(y)\, {\color{orange} \alpha'}\nonumber\\
    &- \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{d}(x') \rho^{e}(x) \rho^{h}(y) U^{d c}(x') U^{b e}(x) U^{a h}(y)\, {\color{orange} \alpha'}\nonumber\\
    &+  \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\, \mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{e}(x') \rho^{h}(y) \rho^{d}(x) U^{d c}(x) U^{b e}(x') U^{a h}(y)\, {\color{red} \alpha} \Bigg]
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,{\color{blue}\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}}\,\int_{x,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik(w'-w)}\,\Bigg[\notag\\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{bc}(w)\,\rho^{a}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(w)\,U^{ca}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\notag\\
& -\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{bc}(x)\,\rho^{a}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ca}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{cb}(w)\,\rho^{a}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(w)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\notag\\
& -\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{dh}(w')\,U^{hb}(w)\,\rho^{e}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{dh}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dae}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{dh}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(w')\,U^{lc}(x)\,U^{ad}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lc}(x')\,U^{eb}(w)\,U^{ad}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x)\notag\\
& -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{dh}(w')\,U^{hb}(w)\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{dh}(x')\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{dh}(x')\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(w')\,U^{dh}(w)\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(w')\,U^{dc}(x')\,U^{al}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{h}(x')\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ec}(x')\,U^{db}(w)\,U^{al}(x)\,\rho^{h}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\Bigg]
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{2\,\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,{\color{blue}\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}}\,\int_{x,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik(w'-w)}\,\Bigg[
\notag\\ &+2\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(w')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\notag\\
&-2\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\notag\\
&-3\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{bl}(w)\,U^{dh}(x)\,U^{ml}(w')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&+2\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dh}(x)\,U^{ml}(w')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&-3\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dl}(z)\,U^{mh}(w')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&+3\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{dl}(w)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{hb}(x)\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&+\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&+2\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dh}(x)\,U^{ml}(x')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&-2\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{hl}(w')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&-2\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&+\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&+\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(x)\,U^{md}(z)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&+\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(z)\,U^{bh}(x)\,U^{el}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
&+3\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ah}(x)\,U^{dl}(w')\,U^{eb}(z)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
&+2\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
&-2\,N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
&-\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{hl}(w)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{md}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
&+\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{hl}(x)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{md}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
&+\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{hl}(w)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
&-\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{hl}(x)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\

\end{align}

\section{The three-\texorpdfstring{$\rho$}{rho} terms cancel}\label{sec:three}
Same test as in \texttt{LL\_terms.pdf}, Sec.~7. Every three-$\rho$ term of the list (your lines, the blue lines, (B), and your $d^3N_a$, $d^3N_b$) is
\begin{itemize}
\item renamed to the frame $w,w',z$, charges $x,y,x'$;
\item summed over the 6 placements of the charges and, for Groups I and III, its c.c.;
\item evaluated with random points, random SU(3) adjoint Wilson lines ($U^{bc}=U^{cb}$), random charges, and $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}=1$.
\end{itemize}

\section{The two-\texorpdfstring{$\rho$}{rho} terms: BFKL}\label{sec:two}
Row by row, the list above equals the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ list of \texttt{LL\_terms.pdf} ($8.8\times10^{-17}$; check B). In each row of Group III Rows III, V, VI and Group II Row IV, the pair (i), (ii) cancels; what is left there is (B), resp.\ $H_4$. The two-$\rho$ terms of that list were added up and compared with BFKL$\otimes$LO kernel by kernel. Row by row they are the genuine two-$\rho$ terms and the commutator terms of the three-$\rho$ terms, $[\rho^a(u),\rho^b(v)]=if^{abc}\rho^c(u)\delta(u-v)$ (orange / purple: 168 / 162):

& +\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x') +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\notag\\
& +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x') -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
In color singlet form, we get
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{1}{N^{2}_{c}-1}\,\log\Big[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\Big]\,\int_{x,,z,w,w'}e^{-ik(w'-w)}\,\langle\rho^{f}(x)\rho^{f}(y) \rangle\,\Bigg[ \notag\\ & \mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,x,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+K(w,w',z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w')\cdot\mathcal K(w-z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(x)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w')\cdot\mathcal K(w-z)\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(x)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(w'-z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(w'-z)\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
where $K(x,y,z) = \frac{(x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}$

This is your App.~E formula,
\begin{align}
\frac{dN}{d^2w\,d^2w'\,dy}\bigg|_{\rm BFKL}={}&-\frac{\alpha_s^2N_c\,\delta Y}{8\pi^6(N_c^2-1)}\int_{x,y,z}\langle\rho^f(x)\rho^f(y)\rangle\Big[
\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')K_D(x,y,z){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\notag\\
&-2\,\mathcal K(z-w)\cdot\mathcal K(z-w')K(x,y,z){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(z-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\big(2K(x,y,z)-K(x,x,z)\big){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(z-w')\big(2K(x,y,z)-K(y,y,z)\big){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big],
\label{eq:BFKLapp}
\end{align}
$K_D=K(x,x,z)-2K(x,y,z)+K(y,y,z)$, $\delta Y=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, $\mathcal N\delta Y\leftrightarrow\frac{\alpha_s^2\delta Y}{8\pi^6(N_c^2-1)}$. Term by term, your singlet form is this, with $\mathcal K(z-w)=-\mathcal K(w-z)$ and $x\leftrightarrow y$ in the last two lines. It agrees with BFKL$\otimes$LO to $8\times10^{-15}$ (\texttt{Final\_evolution\_check.pdf}). Hence
\begin{equation}
\frac{d^3N_{\rm BFKL}}{d^3k}=\frac{dN}{d^2w\,d^2w'\,dy}\bigg|_{\rm BFKL}\otimes\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)},\qquad \delta Y=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}.
\label{eq:BFKLfinal}
\end{equation}

\section{Poles}\label{sec:pole}
With $E(r)=\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\gamma_E+\log\frac{r^2\mu^2}{4}$ and $\mathcal S$ the LO structure (renaming as in \texttt{Final\_evolution\_check.pdf}, Sec.~6.1):
\begin{itemize}
\item The list has two terms with a pole: your Group II Row IV line 1, $+\frac{g^4N_c}{8\pi^4}E(w-w')\mathcal S\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^3k^+}\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, and the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ part of line 2 of \texttt{Other\_terms.tex}, which is exactly minus it. Pointwise, their poles cancel (relative $2.9\times10^{-16}$) and so do their finite parts ($9.3\times10^{-15}$).
\item The removed Group I Row V 2b UV line, $-\frac{g^4N_c}{16\pi^4}\big[E(x-w)+E(x'-w')\big]\mathcal S$ (with c.c.) times $\frac{1}{(2\pi)^3k^+}\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, has a pole of its own. With it the list would not be pole free.
\item The poles $\times\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ of Group I Row II, Group I Row V 2b UV (with E1) and Group II Row IV are in the ratio $1:-2:1$ and cancel (\texttt{LL\_terms.pdf}).
\end{itemize}

\section{Your Group I, II, III files}\label{sec:groups}
Every step of your \texttt{Group\_I.tex}, \texttt{Group\_II.tex}, \texttt{Group\_III.tex} was checked numerically (\texttt{rows\_check/Group\_check.pdf}, with corrected copies). Groups I and III are taken with their complex conjugate, Group II without, in every test here (three $\rho$, two $\rho$, poles).
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{M1 (changes the result), Group I Row V 2b UV}, Eqs.~(1.26), (1.32): the bracket is $\big[2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+2\log(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+})-\frac{11}{6}\big]$, with no $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ (Row V has $0<p^+<k^+$). Your exact Eq.~(1.25) has $2\log\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}-2\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}=2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$. This is the red line here (E1).
\item M2, Group II Row IV, Eq.~(1.16): the phase of the second term is $e^{-ik\cdot(y'-y)\frac{p^++k^+}{k^+}}$ (a typo; Eq.~(1.17) is right).
\item M3, Group III Row III, first line: the overall factor is $-\frac{4}{(2\pi)^3}$, as in Rows V and VI.
\item M4, Group III Row III part 2: the measures are $\int_{w,w',x',y',z,x}$ and, after the $w'$ integral, $\int_{w,x',y',z,x}$ (C5).
\item M5, Group I Row II, Eq.~(1.6): the measure is $\int_{w,w',x'}$.
\item M6: typos ($k\{+\}$ in Group II Eq.~(1.13); brace mismatches in Group I; two commas; a repeated sentence in Group II).
\item Every other step agrees to $10^{-16}$--$5\times10^{-12}$; the plus-prescription steps agree up to $O(\Lambda)$.
\end{itemize}
The rows used here are those of your Group files: \texttt{Other\_terms.tex} $+$ \texttt{BFKL.tex} reproduce them (check A). The latest upload differs from the files of the split only in colour marks and in the Group I Row V 2b UV line (M1).
